# Skills · S · part 99

817 entries, short-read-simulation-from-variant-haplotypes to show-me. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **short-read-simulation-from-variant-haplotypes**: Use when benchmarking haplotype assembly or variant phasing tools requires synthetic reads whose true haplotype origin is known, e.g. from trio-phased 1000 Genomes haplotypes across coverages (10x/30x/50x) and platforms. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-epigenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/epigenomics/v2/leaves/short-read-simulation-from-variant-haplotypes))
- **short-read-splice-aware-alignment**: Use when an lrCaptureSeq (PacBio) isoform catalog needs independent validation: short-read RNA-seq (GSE101986, GSE79416) can confirm splice junctions, and CAGE-seq (DRA002410) can confirm 5' transcription start sites. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-splice-aware-alignment))
- **short-read-to-assembly-mapping**: Use when you have FASTA reads flagged by pal-MEM as containing inverted repeats and per-sample assemblies, and need to anchor those reads genomically and filter candidate ITRs by IS-length proximity before clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-to-assembly-mapping))
- **short-read-to-long-read-alignment**: Use when a hybrid error-correction task requires mapping short reads (e.g. Illumina FASTQ) onto long reads (e.g. PacBio/Sequel FASTA) to localize questionable bases before consensus- or optimization-based correction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-to-long-read-alignment))
- **short-read-variant-based-assembly-polishing**: Use when a long-read (e.g. Oxford Nanopore) assembly has high raw error rates and paired Illumina reads are available to raise base-level accuracy, and when the improvement must be quantified per polishing stage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-variant-based-assembly-polishing))
- **short-read-variant-calling**: Use when you have standard Illumina FASTQ or 10x Linked-Read FASTQ from a GIAB cell line and need benchmarkable SNV/SV call sets to compare against an alternative strategy such as assembly-based variant calling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metagenomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metagenomics/v1/leaves/short-read-variant-calling))
- **short-read-variant-calling-with-clair3**: Use when you have basecalled nanopore reads aligned to hg38 and need to detect SNVs/indels, especially when comparing variant-detection performance across read simulators on chr22 (NA12878) data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-variant-calling-with-clair3))
- **short-read-vs-long-read-coverage-comparison**: Use when both Illumina and ONT cDNA BAM alignments of the same mRNA (e.g. eGFP vaccine construct) are available and you must quantify short-read misalignment at low-complexity poly(A) regions versus long-read coverage. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-vs-long-read-coverage-comparison))
- **short-read-wgs-variant-calling**: Use when starting from aligned short-read Illumina WGS (or WES) BAM/CRAM files for a sequenced parent-child trio and the goal is to produce high-quality de novo variant (DNV) calls rather than standard germline variant sets. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-genomics/tree/HEAD/collections/genomics/v1/leaves/short-read-wgs-variant-calling))
